We present ZERO-APT, a turn-based attacker-defender-judge framework that addresses them within a single architecture. For realism, ZERO-APT embeds a configurable LLM Defender that consumes Sysmon telemetry and detects attacks in real time, exposing the attacker to a live opponent rather than a passive target. For consistency, three architectural mechanisms move causal consistency from unstable LLM reasoning into enforced system architecture: separation of planning from execution, multi-dimensional ReAct feedback, and a hard-constraint-filtered action library. For auditability, a dedicated Judge agent adjudicates each round, maintains global state, and emits structured post-hoc CTI reports that make every decision traceable. We evaluate a Windows Server 2022 post-exploitation prototype across five scenarios with three Defender configurations. ZERO-APT reaches 79\% attack success rate (Aurora 22\%, PentestGPT 39\%), a Causal Consistency Score of 0.860 (Aurora 0.930, Claude Code 0.520), and end-to-end decision auditability through structured CTI reports. We release the benchmark to support evaluation of penetration agents under intelligent defense.

</details>


### [114] [Dimensionality Reduction for Cyberattack Classification: A Comparative Evaluation of PCA and Linear Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05584)
*Nelly Elsayed,Zag ElSayed,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了主成分分析(PCA)和线性预测编码(LPC)两种降维方法在网络攻击检测中的特征压缩效果，实验表明PCA能在大幅压缩下保持分类性能，LPC次之，两种方法均能以最小精度损失实现显著的特征降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维特征表示在网络攻击检测中广泛使用，但增加了计算复杂度，限制了在资源受限环境中的部署，因此需要有效的特征压缩技术。

Method: 对比两种降维方法：主成分分析(PCA)和线性预测编码(LPC)，生成不同维度的压缩特征表示，并在多种分类模型上进行评估。

Result: PCA在激进压缩下仍能保持分类性能；LPC提供了有竞争力的预测表示但性能退化略大；两种方法均能大幅降低特征维度且对分类精度影响极小。

Conclusion: 轻量级特征压缩技术在高效网络安全分析中具有潜力，可在资源受限环境中实现高效部署。

Abstract: High-dimensional feature representations are widely used in machine learning-based cyberattack detection systems. However, they increase computational complexity and may hinder deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we investigate feature compression techniques for cyberattack classification by comparing two dimensionality reduction approaches: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear Predictive Coding (LPC). Compressed feature representations with varying dimensionalities are generated and evaluated across several classification models. Experimental analysis demonstrates that PCA preserves classification performance even under aggressive compression. On the other hand, LPC provides competitive predictive representations with slightly larger performance degradation. The results show that substantial reductions in feature dimensionality can be achieved with minimal impact on classification accuracy, highlighting the potential of lightweight feature compression for efficient cybersecurity analytics.

</details>


### [115] [The Coverage Gap: Chile's Cyber Disclosure Framework versus the USA, EU and UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05594)
*David Mellafe Z*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了"覆盖缺口"（Coverage Gap）概念，用于量化关键基础设施运营商的公开暴露与其漏洞披露能力之间的差距。通过对智利915家"重要运营商"（OIV）的全量普查发现，仅1.7%（16家）发布了可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道，84%存在邮件认证配置错误，约23.5%使用已知漏洞组件。与美英荷等国相比，智利在邮件认证强制令方面落后约8年。文章提出了基于BOD 18-01和英国公共部门DMARC工具包的四阶段路线图，并开源了anci-oiv-resolver工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施运营商的漏洞披露能力是网络安全的基础，但缺乏可量化的跨辖区评估方法。智利2025年新发布的《第21.663号法律》指定了915家OIV，为全量普查提供了独特窗口，此前尚无研究系统性地测量此类运营商的披露能力缺口。

Method: 采用仅基于被动OSINT的方法，符合ISO/IEC 29147:2018标准和智利计算机犯罪安全港条款（Ley 21.459）。对官方目录中约98.7%的OIV进行全量普查，验证其基础披露能力层（Layer 1，即可验证的披露联系渠道），并评估邮件认证配置（SPF/DKIM/DMARC）和已知漏洞组件使用情况。跨辖区基准比较涵盖美国、英国、荷兰、丹麦等国。

Result: 915家OIV中仅16家（1.7%）发布可验证的RFC 9116披露通道；物理世界基础设施运营商（能源、医疗、银行、电信等）中不到10家具有该能力，四大银行和两大电信运营商全部缺失。相比之下，美国联邦文职部门在CISA BOD 18-01下的合规率超过99%。84%的OIV存在邮件认证配置错误，约23.5%使用生命周期终止或已知漏洞组件（Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]）。智利在邮件认证强制令方面落后美国、英国、荷兰约8年，落后丹麦约3年。

Conclusion: 智利关键基础设施运营商的漏洞披露能力存在严重缺口，迫切需要政策干预。文章提出基于国际最佳实践的四阶段路线图，并开源anci-oiv-resolver工具以支持独立复现和持续审计，为政策制定者提供了可操作的改进路径。

Abstract: We introduce the Coverage Gap as a measurable distance between the observable public exposure of critical-infrastructure operators and their declared capability to coordinate vulnerability disclosure. We instantiate it against the 915 Chilean Operadores de Importancia Vital (OIVs -- Operators of Vital Importance) designated by the National Cybersecurity Agency (ANCI) under Ley 21.663 (Resolucion Exenta No. 87, 16 December 2025). Using a passive-only, OSINT-based method consistent with the principles of ISO/IEC 29147:2018 and Chile's computer-crimes safe harbour (Ley 21.459), we conduct a full-universe census of the foundational disclosure-capability layer (Layer 1, verifiable disclosure contact) across approximately 98.7% of the official catalogue. Only 16 of 915 OIVs (1.7%) publish a verifiable RFC 9116 disclosure channel; among operators of physical-world infrastructure -- energy, health, banking, telecommunications, fuel, water, transport, and state administration -- fewer than ten do so, and all four major banks and both telecommunications incumbents lack one entirely. This compares with over 99% adherence in the U.S. federal civilian branch under CISA Binding Operational Directive 18-01. Email-authentication misconfiguration affects 766 of 915 (84%) OIVs, and end-of-life or known-vulnerable stack components an estimated 23.5% (Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]). Cross-jurisdictional benchmarking situates Chile roughly eight years behind the USA, the UK, and the Netherlands on email-authentication mandates, and three years behind Denmark. We propose a four-stage roadmap modelled on BOD 18-01 and the UK Public-Sector DMARC Toolkit, and release the open-source tool anci-oiv-resolver (Apache 2.0) to enable independent reproduction of the OIV-domain mapping that underpins universe-scale auditing.

</details>


### [116] [SlotGCG: Exploiting the Positional Vulnerability in LLMs for Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05609)
*Seungwon Jeong,Jiwoo Jeong,Hyeonjin Kim,Yunseok Lee,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotGCG方法，通过量化提示词中不同插入位置（slots）的脆弱性得分（VSS），选择最易受攻击的位置进行对抗性token插入，从而显著提升大语言模型越狱攻击的成功率，仅需增加200ms预处理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型越狱攻击（如GCG）仅将对抗性token固定在提示词末尾，未探索其他插入位置对攻击效果的影响，需要系统性地研究位置（slots）选择与越狱脆弱性的关系。

Method: 提出脆弱性插槽得分（VSS）量化不同位置的越狱脆弱性；基于VSS开发SlotGCG方法，先评估所有插槽得分，选择最脆弱的插槽进行对抗性token插入，再执行针对性优化攻击。该方法攻击无关，可即插即用于任何优化型攻击。

Result: 在多个模型上，SlotGCG相比GCG基线攻击成功率（ASR）提升14%，收敛更快，对抗防御方法时ASR提升42%，预处理仅增加200ms。

Conclusion: 提示词中不同位置的越狱脆弱性存在显著差异，通过VSS量化并选择最脆弱的插槽进行攻击，可以有效提升越狱攻击性能，且该方法具有通用性和高效性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are widely deployed, identifying their vulnerability through jailbreak attacks becomes increasingly critical. Optimization-based attacks like Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) have focused on inserting adversarial tokens to the end of prompts. However, GCG restricts adversarial tokens to a fixed insertion point (typically the prompt suffix), leaving the effect of inserting tokens at other positions unexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate \emph{slots}, i.e., candidate positions within a prompt where tokens can be inserted. We find that vulnerability to jailbreaking is highly related to the selection of the \emph{slots}. Based on these findings, we introduce the \textit{Vulnerable Slot Score} (VSS) to quantify the positional vulnerability to jailbreaking. We then propose SlotGCG, which evaluates all slots with VSS, selects the most vulnerable slots for insertion, and runs a targeted optimization attack at those slots. Our approach provides a position-search mechanism that is attack-agnostic and can be plugged into any optimization-based attack, adding only 200ms of preprocessing time. Experiments across multiple models demonstrate that SlotGCG significantly outperforms existing methods. Specifically, it achieves 14\% higher Attack Success Rates (ASR) over GCG-based attacks, converges faster, and shows superior robustness against defense methods with 42\% higher ASR than baseline approaches. Our implementation is available at \href{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}

</details>


### [117] [Protecting K-Nearest Neighbor Queries from Location Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05648)
*Zhiyu Sun,Jie Fu,Xinpeng Ling,Huifa Li,Zhili Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了k近邻查询（kNNQ）中固有的位置隐私风险，提出了两种位置推断攻击（GI-LIA和ZO-LIA），并设计了基于差分隐私的DPRS框架，通过带约束扰动区间的拒绝采样机制在隐私保护和查询效用之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kNNQ作为LBS核心组件广泛应用于"附近的人"等功能，但其潜在隐私风险长期被忽视。作者旨在揭示这些风险并提出有效防御方案。

Method: 提出两种攻击方法：几何交集位置推断攻击（GI-LIA）和零阶优化位置推断攻击（ZO-LIA）；设计DPRS差分隐私框架，核心是在约束扰动区间内引入拒绝采样机制以减少过度噪声注入导致的距离失真，并设计私有区间构建算法优化隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在真实空间数据集上的大量实验表明，DPRS在隐私保护和查询效用方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: kNNQ存在严重的位置隐私泄露风险，所提出的DPRS框架能够有效缓解该问题，实现更好的隐私-效用平衡。

Abstract: The k-nearest neighbor query (kNNQ) is a core component of modern location-based services (LBS) and has been widely adopted in popular features such as ``people nearby''. However, its potential privacy risks have long been overlooked. In this work, we present the first two attacks against kNNQ, namely the geometric intersection location inference attack (GI-LIA) and the zero-order optimization location inference attack (ZO-LIA), revealing the inherent location privacy risks posed by kNNQ. To mitigate these privacy risks, we further propose DPRS, a differential privacy framework for kNNQ protection. The core idea of DPRS is to incorporate a rejection sampling mechanism within a constrained perturbation interval, thereby mitigating the distance distortion caused by excessive noise injection. In addition, we design a private interval construction algorithm to construct the perturbation interval, enabling the rejection sampling mechanism to achieve a more favorable trade-off between privacy protection and query utility in kNNQ. Extensive experiments on real-world spatial datasets demonstrate that DPRS outperforms existing methods in both privacy protection and query utility. Our code is available at https://github.com/reanatom/DPRS.

</details>


### [118] [Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics for distributed Infrastructure Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05701)
*Md. Arifur Rahman,B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Iqbal Hossan,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向分布式基础设施系统的认知威胁情报与可解释联邦安全分析框架，该框架集成联邦学习（FL）、可解释人工智能（XAI）和认知网络安全分析，通过在分布式节点上独立训练本地安全模型并仅共享加密模型参数与更新，实现了隐私保护和协作式的网络威胁检测，解决了传统集中式入侵检测在可扩展性、数据隐私、通信开销和AI决策透明度方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式基础设施、云计算、物联网和边缘计算技术的广泛采用，网络攻击面显著扩大，威胁日益复杂。传统集中式入侵检测方法面临可扩展性差、数据隐私风险高、通信开销大以及AI驱动决策过程透明度不足等挑战。

Method: 提出一个认知威胁智能与可解释联邦安全分析框架，集成了联邦学习（FL）、可解释人工智能（XAI）和认知网络安全分析。本地安全模型在分布式节点上独立训练，仅将加密的模型参数和更新通过联邦聚合机制共享，并采用机器学习与深度学习算法（如随机森林、XGBoost、自编码器）来增强智能威胁分析能力。

Result: 该框架通过去中心化学习架构改善了隐私保护，同时降低了通信依赖和集中式安全风险。由于原文为摘要截断，具体实验性能指标结果未完整呈现。

Conclusion: 该集成FL、XAI和认知分析的框架为分布式基础设施提供了一种隐私保护、可解释且协作式的网络威胁检测方案，有效克服了传统集中式方法的局限性。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of distributed infrastructure systems, cloud computing, Internet of Things (IoT) technologies, and edge-based architectures has significantly expanded the cybersecurity attack surface and introduced increasingly sophisticated cyber threats. Conventional centralized intrusion detection approaches often face challenges related to scalability, data privacy, communication overhead, and limited transparency in artificial intelligence-driven decision-making processes. To address these limitations, this study proposes a Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics framework for distributed infrastructure systems. The proposed framework integrates Federated Learning (FL), Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), and cognitive cybersecurity analytics to enable collaborative and privacy-preserving cyber threat detection across distributed network environments. Instead of transmitting sensitive raw network traffic data to centralized servers, local security models are independently trained at distributed nodes, where only encrypted model parameters and updates are shared through a federated aggregation mechanism. This decentralized learning architecture improves privacy protection while reducing communication dependency and centralized security risks. To enhance intelligent threat analysis, the framework incorporates machine learning and deep learning algorithms including Random Forest, XGBoost, Autoencoder

</details>


### [119] [Explainable AI-Driven Cyber Risk Analytics and Model Reliability Assessment for Intelligent Governance of U.S. Critical Infrastructure: An XGBoost and SHAP-Based Intrusion Detection Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05710)
*B. M. Taslimul Haque,Md. Arifur Rahman,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Iqbal Hossan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对美国关键基础设施面临的网络威胁，提出了一种基于机器学习（XGBoost、随机森林、决策树）和可解释人工智能（XAI）的弹性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架，利用CICIDS2017数据集进行入侵检测和风险预测，通过多种性能指标验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国关键基础设施在智能化转型过程中面临日益复杂的网络威胁（如DDoS、勒索软件、APT等），传统网络安全机制难以应对动态变化的攻击环境，亟需开发更具弹性和可解释性的网络风险分析与决策支持框架。

Method: 基于CICIDS2017数据集，采用XGBoost、随机森林和决策树等机器学习分类器进行入侵检测和网络风险预测，并集成可解释人工智能（XAI）技术增强决策透明度和可解释性。通过准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC和假阳性率等多维度指标评估模型性能。

Result: 提出的框架在多个性能指标（准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC、假阳性率）上表现出良好的可靠性和弹性，XAI的集成有效提升了网络安全决策过程的透明度和可解释性。

Conclusion: 所提出的弹性网络风险分析与模型可靠性评估框架能够有效支持美国关键基础设施环境中的智能治理和决策支持，增强对网络风险暴露的应对能力。

Abstract: The increasing penetrations of the critical infrastructure sector in the United States with intelligent digital technologies have greatly increased exposure to advanced cyber adversaries and operational vulnerabilities. AI-powered governance and automated decision-making systems are becoming a key part of the operation of critical infrastructure systems, including energy, healthcare, transportation, financial services, and communication infrastructure, in order to improve efficiency and strategic management. The growing cyber threat environment, such as Distributed Denial of Service (DDos) attacks, botnets, ransomware, and Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) pose significant challenges to infrastructure resilience, cyber security reliability, and governance trustworthiness. In a changing attack landscape and dynamic network environment, traditional cybersecurity mechanisms can often fall short of meeting the evolving needs and protecting critical systems. This study will develop a resilient cyber risk analytics and model reliability assessment framework to support intelligent governance and decision support for cyber risk exposure in the U.S. critical infrastructure environment. This study is based on the CICIDS2017 dataset for the development and testing of intrusion detection system models and cyber risk prediction models based on machine learning. Various classifiers like XGBoost, Random Forest, and Decision Tree are used to detect malicious activities on the network and determine the level of cyber risk. Furthermore, the Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques are integrated to enhance transparency, interpretability, and trust in cybersecurity decision-making processes. The proposed framework presents the reliability and resilience of the model by having various performance measures such as accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, ROC-AUC, and false positive rate.

</details>


### [120] [Hybrid CNN-LSTM Framework for Intelligent Cyber Attack Detection and Prevention in U.S. Critical Digital Infrastructure: A Comparative Machine Learning Evaluation on CSE-CIC-IDS2018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05714)
*Md. Iqbal Hossan,Md. Serajul Kabir Chowdhury Rubel,Md. Arifur Rahman,B. M. Taslimul Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI/ML的智能网络防御系统，利用CSE-CIC-IDS2018数据集和多种机器学习/深度学习模型（随机森林、XGBoost、CNN、LSTM）来检测和预防针对美国数字基础设施的网络攻击，包括DDoS、暴力破解、僵尸网络、渗透攻击和Web攻击等，旨在提升入侵检测的准确性和网络安全韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于签名的入侵检测系统无法有效检测未知和不断变化的网络攻击，而美国数字基础设施（医疗、金融、交通、能源、政府等关键领域）面临的高级网络威胁日益增长，亟需更智能的防御方案。

Method: 使用CSE-CIC-IDS2018真实网络流量数据集，包含多种攻击场景；实施多种ML/DL模型（随机森林、XGBoost、CNN、LSTM）；构建包含数据预处理、特征工程、实时流量监控、智能威胁分类和自动防御机制的综合框架。

Result: 所提出的智能网络防御框架能够有效识别恶意网络行为，提升入侵检测的准确性，并具备实时监控和自动预防能力，从而增强网络安全韧性。

Conclusion: 基于AI/ML的智能网络防御系统能够克服传统方法的局限性，为美国关键数字基础设施提供更有效的实时网络攻击检测和防御能力。

Abstract: Digital infrastructure is growing at a rapid pace in the United States, and as a result, exposure to advanced cyber threats to critical sectors including healthcare, finance, transportation, energy and government systems is growing. The traditional cybersecurity approaches, including signature-based intrusion detection systems, have become less effective against today's cyber attacks, as they are unable to detect unknown and changing attacks in real time. To overcome these constraints, this research suggests a smart cyber-defense system, which utilizes Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) algorithms in the detection and prevention of cyber attacks in the U.S. digital infrastructure. This study uses the CSE-CIC-IDS2018 dataset, which is a realistic network traffic dataset, along with various cyber attack scenarios, including Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS), brute force attacks, botnets, infiltration attacks, and web-based attacks. A number of machine learning and deep learning models such as Random Forest, XGBoost, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks are implemented and evaluated to be used in identifying malicious network behavior and boosting the accuracy of intrusion detection. The framework proposed combines data preprocessing, feature engineering, real-time traffic monitoring, intelligent threat classification with automated prevention mechanisms to build cybersecurity resilience. E

</details>


### [121] [An Embarrassingly Simple Detector for Model Extraction Attacks in Large Language Model API Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05725)
*Shuze Liu,Qianwen Guo,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于良性校准的流量窗口分布测试方法用于LLM模型窃取检测，利用最大均值差异（MMD）统计量比较查询语义分布与历史良性流量，在多个场景下达到0.3%假阳性率、100%纯攻击者检出率和95.1%平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过托管API部署时面临模型窃取威胁，现有检测方法多聚焦于单查询异常评分或纯良性vs攻击者的用户场景，缺乏对混合多用户真实流量的有效检测方案。

Method: 将模型窃取检测建模为良性校准的流量窗口分布测试：将输入查询嵌入语义空间，使用MMD统计量比较聚合查询分布与历史良性流量的偏差，仅通过良性vs良性对比设置决策阈值。

Result: 在14个攻击者-正常查询对、4种提取场景下，与PRADA、SEAT、CAP、DATE、边际马氏距离等基线相比，MMD达到0.3%良性FPR、100.0%纯攻击者TPR、90.5%平均攻击者TPR、95.1%平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 良性校准的分布测试是LLM API流量中模型窃取检测的强经验基线，适用于用户级和混合多用户场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed through hosted APIs, making model extraction a practical threat to model ownership and service security. However, individual extraction queries often resemble benign requests, and existing evaluations often focus on single-query anomaly scoring or pure benign-versus-attacker user settings. We formulate model extraction monitoring as benign-calibrated traffic-window distribution testing and show that an embarrassingly simple detector is effective: embed incoming queries into a semantic space and test whether their aggregate distribution deviates from historical benign traffic. We instantiate the detector with maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), using only benign-vs-benign comparisons to set the decision threshold. We evaluate on fourteen attacker-normal query pairs from four extraction scenarios and compare with adapted PRADA, SEAT, CAP, DATE, and marginal Mahalanobis baselines. Across three random seeds, MMD achieves 0.3% benign FPR, 100.0% pure-attacker TPR, 90.5% average TPR over attacker fractions, and 95.1% balanced accuracy. These results show that benign-calibrated distribution testing is a strong empirical baseline for model extraction detection in both user-level and mixed multi-user LLM API traffic. Code is released at: https://github.com/LabRAI/mmd-llm-mea-detection.

</details>


### [122] [Membrane: A Self-Evolving Contrastive Safety Memory for LLM Agent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05743)
*Minseok Choi,Seungbin Yang,Dongjin Kim,Subin Kim,Jungmin Son,Yunseung Lee,Jaegul Choo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Membrane——一种基于对比安全记忆的自演化护栏，通过构建对比记忆单元同时识别有害查询及其相似的良性变体，在不重训练的情况下持续适应新型越狱攻击，在多个基准测试中实现最高F1分数，并将良性拒绝率降至7-14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全护栏面临两大困境：微调安全分类器无法适应不断演化的越狱攻击，而基于记忆的护栏则倾向于过度拒绝与已知攻击相似的良性查询。亟需一种既能适应新型攻击又能精确区分有害与良性查询的防护机制。

Method: 构建对比安全记忆(CSM)单元，每个单元配对两个条件——阻断有害查询的条件和允许相似良性查询的条件。系统通过蒸馏每次有害交互及其良性对应物，自动演化CSM，按攻击策略索引的对比单元可泛化到同机制的不同主题变体。推理时，检索到的单元作为安全决策的上下文依据。

Result: 在HarmBench和AgentHarm上，Membrane在全部六种越狱攻击中均取得最高F1分数。AgentHarm上良性拒绝率仅7-14%，远低于此前方法的28-85%。跨攻击迁移下记忆单元保持87-88% F1，且在记忆中毒攻击下保持稳定。

Conclusion: Membrane通过对比安全记忆机制，在无需重训练的条件下实现了对不断演变的越狱攻击的自适应防护，同时显著降低了良性查询的过度拒绝率，在鲁棒性和精确性之间取得了优异平衡。

Abstract: Despite advances in safety alignment, large language models remain vulnerable to continuously evolving jailbreaks. Existing fine-tuned safety classifiers cannot adapt to these evolving attacks, while adaptive memory-based guardrails tend to over-refuse benign queries that resemble stored attacks. We propose Membrane, a self-evolving guardrail built on Contrastive Safety Memory (CSM): each cell pairs the conditions for blocking a harmful query with those for permitting a superficially similar benign request. Without retraining, Membrane evolves CSM by distilling each harmful interaction and its benign counterpart into a contrastive cell indexed by the underlying attack strategy, so that one cell generalizes across topical variants of the same mechanism. At inference, retrieved cells serve as grounding context for precise safety decisions. Across model-level safety on HarmBench and agent-level safety on AgentHarm, Membrane achieves the highest F1 on all six jailbreak attacks. Notably, benign refusal on AgentHarm stays at 7-14%, well below the 28-85% range of prior guards. Memory cells also retain 87-88% F1 under cross-attack transfer and remain stable under memory poisoning.

</details>


### [123] [An Improved CNN-LSTM Based Intrusion Detection System for IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05776)
*Mohammad Tariq Ikhlas,Pohanyar Khowaja Khil,Malik Muhammad Mueed Aslam,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的CNN-LSTM入侵检测模型，结合多类分类、数据集集成和时间特征学习，在物联网网络流量数据上实现了约97%的检测准确率，有效捕获了网络流量的空间和时间特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网设备快速普及，网络安全威胁显著增加，亟需高效的入侵检测系统来保护网络环境。

Method: 提出一种改进的CNN-LSTM模型，结合卷积神经网络（捕获空间特征）和循环神经网络（捕获时间特征），采用多类分类和数据集集成策略进行入侵检测。

Result: 在物联网网络流量数据上，模型达到了约97%的准确率，能够有效检测多种攻击类别，且训练和验证性能稳定。

Conclusion: 该CNN-LSTM混合模型通过同时学习网络流量的时空特征，显著提升了物联网环境下的入侵检测能力。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of IoT devices, security concerns have dramatically escalated and intrusion detection systems have become critical for protecting networked environments. This paper presents an improved CNN-LSTM based intrusion detection model that combines multi-class classification, dataset integration, and temporal feature learning to enhance detection performance in IoT networks. Using network traffic data, the proposed approach is evaluated on intrusion detection tasks and achieves an accuracy of approximately 97%. Experimental results demonstrate that the model effectively detects multiple attack categories while maintaining stable training and validation performance. The integration of convolutional and recurrent neural network components enables the framework to capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of network traffic, improving overall intrusion detection capability in IoT environments.

</details>


### [124] [TinyML-Driven Cybersecurity for Autonomous Spacecraft: Latency-Accuracy Analysis for SPARTA RF and Cyber Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05779)
*Van Le,Trevor Tran,Tan Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究TinyML兼容的经典机器学习模型（随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM、MLP）在航天器自主检测射频网络威胁中的延迟-精度权衡，基于SPARTA攻击模型和物理信息理论分析，发现逻辑回归在微秒级推理下仅比随机森林低1%精度，适合作为星载自主基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器需要快速、轻量、可靠的在轨射频网络威胁检测能力，现有方法在计算资源和检测性能之间难以取得平衡。

Method: 基于SPARTA攻击模型，对随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM和MLP四种TinyML兼容模型进行理论分析（计算复杂度、VC维、Lipschitz连续性、延迟缩放），并在通过BandErasure、FakeNR、NoiseBurst生成的对抗性RF频谱图上进行实证测量。

Result: 逻辑回归在微秒级推理延迟下，精度仅比随机森林低1%，是有效的TinyML基线模型。

Conclusion: 逻辑回归适合星载自主检测；未来可通过更丰富的特征编码器和多时间尺度学习架构进一步提升航天器网络安全能力。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft require rapid, lightweight, and reliable onboard detection of cyber-RF threats. Using the SPARTA attack model, we analyze the latency-accuracy trade-offs of TinyML-compatible classical models -- Random Forest, Logistic Regression, SVM, and MLP -- for detecting uplink jamming, Fake-NR spoofing, payload manipulation, ground-segment compromise, and unauthorized command injection. We present a physics-informed theoretical analysis of each model's computational complexity, VC dimension, Lipschitz continuity, and latency scaling, supported by empirical measurements on adversarial RF spectrograms generated via BandErasure, FakeNR, and NoiseBurst corruption modes. Results show that Logistic Regression achieves microsecond-level inference with only a 1\% accuracy drop relative to Random Forest, making it an effective TinyML baseline for onboard autonomy. The study also identifies opportunities for advancing spacecraft cybersecurity through richer feature encoders and multi-timescale learning architectures, building on recent progress in edge intelligence and trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [125] [SentinelRAG: Synthetic Sentinel Knowledge for RAG Database Copyright Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05787)
*Tsun On Kwok,Xi Yang,Ki Sen Hung,Chang Liu,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SentinelRAG提出了一种针对RAG数据库的水印框架，通过注入风格一致但虚构的知识条目，在仅0.1%注入率下实现p<10^{-5}的统计显著检测，同时大幅降低误检率并保持对合法查询的干扰可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG数据库水印方法存在两难困境：要么注入真实实体间的虚构关系，污染知识库并引入错误信息；要么嵌入脆弱的词汇模式，容易被对抗性改写移除。需要一种既能保护版权又不影响知识库质量的方法。

Method: 提出SentinelRAG框架，核心思想是向RAG数据库中注入风格一致但完全虚构的知识条目。这些虚构条目描述的是不存在的实体，因此合法用户查询几乎不会检索到它们；只有数据所有者通过精心设计的定向探针才能可靠触发这些水印，从而实现所有权证明。

Result: 在四个数据集（2.9k到8.8M文档）上，仅需0.1%的注入率即可在所有配置下实现p<10^{-5}的统计显著检测。相比现有方法，显著降低了误检率，且对合法用户查询的干扰可忽略不计。

Conclusion: SentinelRAG通过引入风格一致但虚构的知识条目作为水印，在保护RAG数据库版权的同时，有效避免了传统方法的信息污染和高误检问题，实现了检测效果与知识库质量之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Protecting proprietary RAG databases from unauthorized redistribution is challenging: existing watermarking methods either inject fabricated relations between real entities, polluting the knowledge base with misinformation, or embed fragile lexical patterns that adversarial paraphrasing easily removes. We propose SentinelRAG, a watermarking framework that embeds style-consistent but fictitious knowledge entries into the RAG database. Our key insight is that synthetic knowledge describing fictitious entities is unlikely to be retrieved by legitimate queries, yet can be reliably triggered through targeted probes known only to the data owner. Experiments on four datasets ranging from 2.9k to 8.8M documents demonstrate that SentinelRAG achieves statistically significant detection $p < 10^{-5}$ across all tested configurations at only a 0.1% injection rate. Compared to the state-of-the-art, our method significantly reduces the false detection rate while maintaining negligible interference with legitimate user queries.

</details>


### [126] [GCD: Garbled, Corrected, Demonstrandum -- Fixing and Proving Go's Extended GCD Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05796)
*Linard Arquint*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文验证了Go标准库中'extendedGCD'实现的正确性，发现了两个与BoringSSL实现的关键偏差，并修复了其中一个导致24%性能提升的偏差，同时通过形式化验证（Gobra和Lean）证明了修复后算法的正确性和终止性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go标准库中的'extendedGCD'实现虽声称直接移植自BoringSSL，但其正确性未经验证，而该函数在RSA密钥对生成中扮演关键角色，任何错误都可能导致严重的安全隐患。

Method: 使用Gobra（Go语言的演绎程序验证器）进行代码级形式化验证，针对非线性算术的关键引理使用Lean证明辅助工具，并将Lean证明导入Gobra中完成整体验证。

Result: 发现了两个偏差：系数更新方式的偏差和输入域范围的偏差。修复系数更新偏差后平均性能提升24%，并扩展了原有证明以覆盖更大的输入域。

Conclusion: 即使经过充分审查的代码也可能存在微妙缺陷；形式化验证是发现此类缺陷的有效工具；AI智能体可以通过迭代优化基于验证器错误信息的不变量和引理来辅助验证过程。

Abstract: We verify the 'extendedGCD' implementation in Go's standard library ('crypto/internal/fips140/bigmod'), which plays a crucial role in the generation of RSA key pairs. Even though the Go implementation is supposedly a direct port from BoringSSL's implementation, we uncovered two deviations that each break the algorithm's invariants: (1) the Go implementation deviates in the way coefficients are updated, and (2) it permits a larger input domain. We address both deviations; the first by fixing the Go implementation, which results in an on average 24% speedup, and the second deviation by porting an existing proof for BoringSSL and extending it to cover the larger input domain. We prove correctness and termination of the fixed Go implementation using Gobra, a deductive program verifier for Go. Where necessary, we used Lean to prove key lemmata on non-linear arithmetic, which we import into Gobra.
  Our verification effort reveals three key insights: subtle bugs can slip into even well-reviewed code with surprising ease; formal verification is a powerful tool for uncovering them; and AI agents can facilitate the verification process by iteratively refining invariants and lemmata based on Gobra's error messages.

</details>


### [127] [PriSrv: Privacy-Enhanced and Highly Usable Service Discovery in Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05821)
*Yang Yang,Robert H. Deng,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,HweeHwa Pang,Minming Huang,Rui Shi,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PriSrv，一种基于匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）的私有服务发现协议，通过双层匹配架构实现细粒度认证策略控制，支持选择性属性披露和多次使用不可关联性，并在EAP、mDNS、BLE、AirDrop等主流无线框架上验证了互操作性，在主流移动设备上实现亚秒级安全发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议隐私保护能力有限，会泄露敏感设备信息并暴露攻击路径，亟需在不牺牲可用性的前提下增强隐私保障。

Method: 提出PriSrv协议，核心是匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）原语，其双层匹配架构（外层基于公开属性过滤不匹配实体，内层基于选择性披露的私有属性进行双向认证）；设计快速匿名凭证（FAC）方案，提供常量大小凭证和高效验证。

Result: 在桌面、笔记本、智能手机和树莓派平台上，PriSrv在主流移动设备上实现亚秒级安全发现；与EAP、mDNS、BLE、AirDrop等无线框架成功集成；通过了详尽的形式化安全证明和性能评估。

Conclusion: PriSrv在增强隐私保证的同时保持了高可用性，为无线通信中的服务发现提供了一种兼具安全性和实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: Service discovery is essential in wireless communications. However, existing protocols provide limited privacy protection, leaking sensitive device information and opening routes to network attacks. This paper proposes a private service discovery protocol, called PriSrv, which enables both service providers and clients to specify fine-grained authentication policies before establishing connections. PriSrv achieves this via a dual-layer matching architecture: an outer layer filters mismatched entities using public attributes, while an inner layer handles mutual authentication using selectively disclosed private attributes. As a core component, we introduce the primitive of anonymous credential-based matchmaking encryption (ACME), which enables dual-layer matching in a single step to achieve bilateral policy control, selective attribute disclosure, and multi-show unlinkability. To instantiate ACME, we design a fast anonymous credential (FAC) scheme providing constant-size credentials and efficient verification. We demonstrate PriSrv's interoperability by integrating it with popular wireless frameworks including EAP, mDNS, BLE, and AirDrop. Detailed formal security proofs and extensive performance evaluations across desktop, laptop, smartphone, and Raspberry Pi platforms demonstrate that PriSrv provides enhanced privacy guarantees with high usability, achieving secure discovery in less than one second on mainstream mobile devices.

</details>


### [128] [Towards Worst-case Hardness for Low-Noise LPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05834)
*Divesh Aggarwal,Rishav Gupta,Hai Hoang Nguyen,Kel Zin Tan,Prashant Nalini Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的最坏情况到平均情况归约方法，通过要求计算不可区分性而非统计平滑性，将LPN问题的平均情况困难性归约到最坏情况下的解码和区分问题，首次通过最坏情况归约获得了Alekhnovich公钥加密所需参数区间（逆多项式噪声率n^{-α}, α<1）的LPN困难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPN问题是密码学的基础假设，但其平均情况困难性能否基于最坏情况复杂度假设（如LWE那样）是一个核心开放问题。现有归约[BLVW19, YZ21]依赖线性码的统计平滑，这限制了噪声率至多1/2 - 1/poly(n)，无法支持公钥密码应用。

Method: 不要求生成矩阵的随机稀疏行组合在统计上接近均匀分布，而只要求它们在计算上不可区分。通过这种"双赢"结构，证明任何高效的LPN求解器可转化为一对高效算法(S, D)：对任意矩阵A，要么S能从随机噪声中解码A生成的码字，要么D能区分对偶码的随机噪声陪集与均匀分布。

Result: 在适当参数下，得到了逆多项式噪声率n^{-α}（任意常数α<1）的LPN平均情况困难性，前提是假设最坏情况下同时难以解码码字和区分对偶码的噪声陪集。特别地，当α=1/2时，归约给出了Alekhnovich公钥加密所需参数区间的LPN困难性——这是此前最坏情况归约无法达到的。

Conclusion: 通过计算不可区分性替代统计平滑，突破了现有最坏情况到平均情况归约的噪声率瓶颈，为基于LPN的公钥密码提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The hardness of the Learning Parity with Noise (LPN) problem is a foundational assumption in cryptography, forming the basis of constructions ranging from symmetric-key primitives to public-key encryption and beyond. A central open question is whether the average-case hardness of LPN can be based on worst-case complexity assumptions, as has been achieved for the analogous Learning With Errors (LWE) problem.
  Existing worst-case-to-average-case reductions for LPN [BLVW19, YZ21] rely on statistical smoothing of linear codes, which inherently limits the resulting average-case hardness to noise rates as large as $1/2 - 1/\mathrm{poly}(n)$, which is insufficient for public-key applications.
  We explore a new approach towards obtaining such reductions: rather than requiring that random sparse combinations of the rows of the generator matrix of a code be statistically close to uniform, we only require that they be computationally indistinguishable from uniform. This leads to a clean win-win structure: we show that any efficient LPN solver can be transformed into a pair of efficient algorithms $(S, D)$ such that for every matrix $A$ of appropriate dimensions over $\mathbb{F}_2$, either $S$ decodes the code generated by $A$ from random noise, or $D$ distinguishes random noisy codewords of the dual of this code from uniform.
  By instantiating this reduction with appropriate parameters, we obtain the average-case hardness of LPN with inverse-polynomial noise rate $n^{-α}$ for any constant $α< 1$, assuming the worst-case simultaneous hardness of decoding a code from random noise and distinguishing random noisy codewords of its dual from uniform. In particular, setting $α= 1/2$, our reduction yields LPN hardness in the parameter regime required for Alekhnovich's construction of public-key encryption [Ale03], a regime that was previously inaccessible via worst-case reductions.

</details>


### [129] [GenTI: Benchmarking LLMs for Autonomous IDPS Rule Generation for Unseen Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05844)
*Hassan Jalil Hadi,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出GenTI框架和GTI数据集，利用LLM自动生成IDPS规则，覆盖15万+检测/防御规则和5万YARA规则，通过CoT推理和CoVe验证，实现89.4%的复合规则质量分数，将未知攻击检测率从45%提升至87.4%，假阳性率从8.5%降至2.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的IDPS依赖人工签名，难以适应新兴和零日威胁；公开数据集仅关注流量分类，缺乏支持自动规则合成和防御逻辑的结构化信息。

Method: 构建GTI数据集（15万Snort/Suricata/Emerging Threats规则+5万YARA规则，含协议行为、负载签名、上下文关系、CTI映射和响应类型标注）；设计基于LLM的流水线，通过结构化提示工程、CoT推理和CoVe验证循环（语法、语义、安全三个层面）将分析人员提示和代表性负载转化为可部署规则，并在Snort/Suricata上实时执行评估。

Result: GenTI实现89.4%的复合规则质量分数，94.8%的CTI覆盖率，未知攻击检测率从45%提升至87.4%，假阳性率从8.5%降至2.3%。

Conclusion: GenTI建立了首个将规则级CTI与LLM自动化紧密结合的大规模基准，实现了自适应、自我演进的IDPS。

Abstract: Rule-based Intrusion Detection and Prevention Systems (IDPS) offer precise attack detection as well as mitigation, however their manually crafted, signature-driven rules limit adaptability to emerging and zero-day threats. Additionally, existing public datasets (e.g., CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15) focus on traffic classification and provide little structured information to support automatic rule synthesis or prevention logic. To address this gap, we propose Generative Thread Intelligence (GenTI) \footnote{GenTI refers to the proposed framework, and GTI refers to the dataset.} an LLM-driven benchmark for automatic generation of IDPS rules targeting unseen attacks. The dataset (GTI) aggregates over 150k detection and prevention rules from Snort, Suricata, Emerging Threats, as well as 50k YARA, each annotated with protocol behavior, payload signatures, contextual relationships, mappings to Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI), along with actionable response types (alert, drop, reject). Moreover, on top of this corpus we design an LLM-based pipeline that transforms analyst prompts and representative payloads into deployable rules via structured prompt engineering, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, as well as a Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) loop for syntactic, semantic, and security validation. The generated rules are executed in real time on (Snort/Suricata) and evaluated by syntax accuracy, semantic similarity, CTI coverage, security effectiveness as well as unseen attacks detection. Furthermore, our GenTI instantiation achieves a composite rule-quality score of 89.4\%, with 94.8\% CTI coverage, improving unseen attacks detection from 45\% to 87.4\% and reducing the false-positive rate from 8.5\% to 2.3\%. Overall, GenTI establishes the first large-scale benchmark that tightly couples rule-level CTI with LLM-based automation, enabling adaptive, self-evolving IDPS.

</details>


### [130] [PriSrv+: Privacy and Usability-Enhanced Wireless Service Discovery with Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05902)
*Yang Yang,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,Pengfei Wu,Rui Shi,Minming Huang,Jian Weng,HweeHwa Pang,Robert H. Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PriSrv+ 是一个面向现代无线网络和资源受限环境的高级隐私与可用性增强的服务发现协议，通过提出首个支持无界属性集合的表达性访问控制策略的匹配加密方案 FEME，在加密/解密速度、密文大小和通信开销上相比 PriSrv 实现了数倍优化，并具备形式化安全证明与跨平台性能验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议（包括 PriSrv）在表达能力、隐私保护、可扩展性和效率方面存在关键局限，难以满足 5G 和 IoT 等现代无线网络中资源受限设备对灵活、高效且隐私保护的动态服务发现需求。

Method: 提出 Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME)，这是首个支持无界属性集合（任意字符串作为属性）的表达性访问控制策略的匹配加密方案。在此基础上构建 PriSrv+，兼容 mDNS、BLE 和 Wi-Fi 等主流无线协议，并对加密、解密、密文大小和通信协议进行系统优化。

Result: 相比 PriSrv，PriSrv+ 加密速度提升 7.62 倍，解密速度提升 6.23 倍，密文大小减少 87.33%；服务广播通信成本降低 87.33%，匿名互认证通信成本降低 86.64%。形式化安全证明验证了 FEME 和 PriSrv+ 的安全性，多平台评估表明其在性能、可扩展性和效率上优于现有协议。

Conclusion: PriSrv+ 通过 FEME 方案在表达能力、隐私保护和效率之间取得了更好的平衡，为资源受限环境下的无线网络服务发现提供了更安全、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Service discovery is a fundamental process in wireless networks, enabling devices to find and communicate with services dynamically, and is critical for the seamless operation of modern systems like 5G and IoT. This paper introduces PriSrv+, an advanced privacy and usability-enhanced service discovery protocol for modern wireless networks and resource-constrained environments. PriSrv+ builds upon PriSrv (NDSS'24), by addressing critical limitations in expressiveness, privacy, scalability, and efficiency, while maintaining compatibility with widely-used wireless protocols such as mDNS, BLE, and Wi-Fi.
  A key innovation in PriSrv+ is the development of Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME), the first matchmaking encryption scheme capable of supporting expressive access control policies with an unbounded attribute universe, allowing any arbitrary string to be used as an attribute. FEME significantly enhances the flexibility of service discovery while ensuring robust message and attribute privacy. Compared to PriSrv, PriSrv+ optimizes cryptographic operations, achieving 7.62* faster for encryption and 6.23* faster for decryption, and dramatically reduces ciphertext sizes by 87.33%. In addition, PriSrv+ reduces communication costs by 87.33% for service broadcast and 86.64% for anonymous mutual authentication compared with PriSrv. Formal security proofs confirm the security of FEME and PriSrv+. Extensive evaluations on multiple platforms demonstrate that PriSrv+ achieves superior performance, scalability, and efficiency compared to existing state-of-the-art protocols.

</details>


### [131] [Exploring the connection between coding habits and cognitive styles in malware developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05945)
*Vasilis Vouvoutsis,Constantinos Patsakis,Fran Casino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过静态应用安全测试和软件度量分析大量泄露的恶意软件源码，发现恶意软件比良性软件代码规模更小、文档更少、圈复杂度更高、抽象机制使用更少，且存在更多安全漏洞，反映了其开发策略以权宜、隐蔽和规避为导向，而非长期可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件研究主要关注方法、结果和影响，忽视了攻击者的开发策略与行为模式。本文旨在通过代码结构分析揭示恶意软件开发者的行为特征和战略选择。

Method: 收集大量泄露的恶意软件源码与精心挑选的良性开源软件，应用静态应用安全测试（SAST）并计算多种软件度量指标，基于认知心理学和犯罪学理论解释代码结构差异作为行为指标。

Result: 恶意软件代码规模更小、文档更少、每函数圈复杂度更高，类与闭包等抽象机制使用减少；安全漏洞分析显示恶意软件存在良性代码通常避免的问题类型，表明安全开发实践投入极少。

Conclusion: 代码度量可作为行为信号和战略选择的代理指标，定量软件分析能够丰富行为网络安全研究，为网络犯罪者的行为画像提供新途径。

Abstract: Malware research primarily studies the results, the methods, and the impact. Even from an offensive security perspective, what is examined is the method, not the development strategy of the offender. This study investigates the behavioral signatures and coding patterns embedded in the malware source code. By analyzing a large corpus of leaked malware code and comparing it with carefully selected benign open-source software, we apply static application security testing and compute multiple software metrics. Based on cognitive psychology and criminological theories, our work interprets differences in code structure and quality as behavioral indicators, reflecting distinct motivational structures, risk tolerances, and development strategies of malware authors compared to benign software developers. Our findings reveal that malware code is generally smaller, less documented, and exhibits higher cyclomatic complexity per function, with reduced use of abstraction mechanisms such as classes and closures. Vulnerability analysis further reveals that malware exhibits more issues of the types that benign code typically avoids, suggesting a minimal investment in secure development practices. These patterns imply a development style optimized for expedience, operational secrecy, and evasion rather than long-term maintainability. Nonetheless, the code quality metrics indicate that it does not deviate significantly from benign software enough to be distinctive. By framing code metrics as proxies for behavioral signals and strategic choices, we demonstrate how quantitative software analysis can enrich behavioral cybersecurity research, offering new insights into the practices and priorities of malware developers. Our results pave the way for further research in the behavioral profiling of cyber offenders.

</details>


### [132] [AttackPathGNN: Cross-function vulnerability detection in smart contracts using state interference graphs and conjunction pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05986)
*Gabriela Dobrita,Simona-Vasilica Oprea,Adela Bara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AttackPathGNN是一种图神经网络，通过构建状态干扰图（连接共享可变存储的函数对）和合取池化（可微分的AND聚合器），将智能合约漏洞检测从单函数的语法模式匹配转变为对显式攻击路径的推理，在多个基准测试上达到92.3% F1，并能生成结构化修复报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的Solidity智能合约检测器将漏洞检测简化为单函数内的语法模式匹配，但许多重大攻击（The DAO、Cream Finance）并非源于单个函数，而是函数间关系和多条件组合所致。因此需要一种能推理函数间攻击路径的检测方法。

Method: 提出AttackPathGNN，包含两个核心设计：(1)状态干扰图（SIG），通过带类型和权重的边连接所有共享可变存储的函数对，并通过五条件谓词定义有向重入路径边；(2)合取池化（Conjunction Pooling），一种可微分的AND聚合器，对八个命名的利用前提进行聚合，其log-sigmoid形式使得任意单个缓解措施（重入锁、访问控制修饰符或SafeMath）存在时，单函数漏洞分数会骤降。

Result: 在五次独立训练中，AttackPathGNN在SmartBugs Wild测试集上达到92.3±0.2% F1（假阴性率4.3±0.3%），在人工标注的SmartBugs Curated基准上检测率90.8±2.5%；在DASP10分类中，6个类别达到100%召回，重入检测达98.7±1.8%。

Conclusion: AttackPathGNN通过显式建模函数间攻击路径和可微分的条件聚合，有效提升了智能合约漏洞检测的可解释性和准确性，每次预测附带结构化修复报告，将检测结果转化为可操作的审计发现。

Abstract: Existing learning-based detectors for Solidity smart-contracts reduce vulnerability detection to syntactic pattern matching within single functions, yet many of the most consequential exploits (The DAO, Cream Finance) exist not in any individual function but in the relationship between functions and in the combination of conditions that made the attack feasible. Thus, we propose AttackPathGNN, a graph neural network (GNN) that reframes detection as reasoning over explicit attack paths. Two architectural choices distinguish it from prior GNN-based detectors: (1)a State Interference Graph that links every pair of functions sharing mutable storage through typed, weighted edges and through directed reentrancy-path edges defined by an explicit five-condition predicate; (2)conjunction pooling, a differentiable AND-aggregator over eight named exploit preconditions whose log-sigmoid form causes the per-function exploit score to collapse whenever any single mitigation (a reentrancy guard, an access-control modifier or SafeMath) is in place. Across five independent training runs, AttackPathGNN attains 92.3+/-0.2% F1 on the SmartBugs Wild held-out test partition (4.3+/-0.3% false-negative rate, 90.8+/-2.5% detection rate on the independently human-labelled SmartBugs Curated benchmark), recovering 6/10 DASP10 categories at 100% on every seed and Reentrancy at 98.7+/-1.8%. Each prediction is emitted with a structured remediation report, turning each verdict into an actionable, function-level audit finding.

</details>


### [133] [Cheating in Multiplayer Online Games: a Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06013)
*Hugo Bertin,Marc Dacier,Yérom-David Bromberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个包含网络流干扰作弊（如aimbots和wallhacks）的多人在线游戏数据集，该数据集通过结合真实玩家与自动化游戏客户端的游戏会话记录，并利用实验框架收集网络层和应用层日志，旨在为学术界和工业界提供作弊检测研究的基础数据资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流干扰类作弊难以检测且缺乏代表性标注数据集，现有作弊检测研究受限于数据不足，亟需一个包含此类作弊标注的公开数据集来推动相关检测技术的发展。

Method: 利用一个结合多人在线游戏与插件的实验框架，该插件既能复现作弊攻击又能收集网络层和应用层的日志，通过真实玩家与自动化游戏客户端进行游戏会话，显式记录作弊行为。

Result: 构建了首个包含网络流干扰作弊日志的数据集，同时也涵盖了aimbots和wallhacks等常见作弊类型；数据集被设计为可演进的，允许他人使用该框架创建自己的数据轨迹进行扩展。

Conclusion: 该数据集为在线游戏作弊检测研究提供了重要的基准数据资源，填补了网络流干扰作弊标注数据的空白，并支持社区共同扩展和演化。

Abstract: Cheating poses a significant threat to the Multiplayer Online Games (MOG) industry by degrading player satisfaction and undermining the fairness in competitive gaming. Despite efforts to develop mitigation techniques, cheating remains difficult to detect and prevent in practice. In particular, a class of cheats based on network flow disruption remains unsolvable. To find out how to detect such attacks we need access to representative labelled data. However, no such dataset exists.
  To address this gap, we leverage an experimental framework that combines a multiplayer online game with a plug-in capable of both reproducing cheating attacks and collecting logs at two levels: network and application-layer.
  This paper presents a dataset compiling records of game sessions played by both real players and automated game clients, with cheating actions explicitly logged. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first dataset that provides logs of network flow disruption cheats. While it includes such network-based cheats, it is not limited to them and also contains records of more commonly studied cheats, such as aimbots and wallhacks. This dataset can be used by researchers in academia and industry seeking to develop cheating detection mechanisms for online games. Furthermore, it is designed to be evolutive and can be enriched by others creating their own data traces with the proposed framework.

</details>


### [134] [RedEdit: Agentic Red-Teaming of Image Safety Classifiers via MCTS-Guided Photo-Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06140)
*Weilin Lin,Ziqi Lin,Zhenxing Zhou,Jianze Li,Tong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了RedEdit，一种基于黑盒的红队攻击框架，通过将照片编辑逃逸建模为编辑工具序列的组合搜索问题，利用VLM提案器和MCTS规划器协同工作，平均仅需不到2次编辑即可使76.2%的不安全图像绕过检测器，同时保留93.0%的恶意语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像安全分类器在面对用户风格的恶意图像编辑时的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，而这种行为在现实场景中非常普遍却难以复现。

Method: 提出RedEdit框架，将照片编辑逃逸建模为组合搜索问题，采用基于VLM的提案器生成语义定向的候选编辑操作，并结合MCTS规划器优先探索有前景的编辑路径并从无效路径回溯。

Result: 在UnsafeBench上，平均不到2次编辑即可使76.2%的不安全图像逃逸检测器，同时保留93.0%的恶意语义。

Conclusion: 揭示了现有内容审核系统在面对针对性图像编辑时的严重漏洞，呼吁社区关注这一被忽视的实际威胁。

Abstract: Image safety classifiers serve as a critical component of contemporary content moderation systems on the internet. However, their resilience against user-style malicious image editing remains underexplored. Such behaviors are highly prevalent in daily scenarios but difficult to fully reproduce. To explore this vulnerability, we introduce RedEdit, a novel black-box red-teaming agent that formulates photo-editing evasion as a combinatorial search problem over edit-tool sequences. It adopts a Vision-Language-Model (VLM)-based proposer to generate semantically targeted candidate edits and a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) planner to prioritize promising edit paths while backtracking from ineffective ones. Together, the proposer and planner instantiate two key capabilities of human attackers, i.e., domain knowledge and iterative backtracking, respectively, to reproduce this practical threat. Our extensive experiments on UnsafeBench reveal profound systemic vulnerabilities: fewer than two edits on average enable 76.2% of unsafe images to evade detectors, while retaining 93.0% malicious semantics, meaning that such manipulated content remains perceptually malicious to humans while easily bypassing automated moderation. We therefore appeal to the community for more attention to this overlooked practical threat.

</details>


### [135] [Opportunities and Challenges in Securely Reusing and Repurposing Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06181)
*Adelin Roty,Jan Tobias Mühlberg,Jean-François Determe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能手机在原始生态系统之外被重新利用时，其硬件安全机制（如启动链完整性、可信执行环境隔离、硬件绑定密钥保护）是否仍然有效。通过对PinePhone的实验，发现供应商锁定的安全机制严重阻碍了设备的再利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量废弃智能手机可被重新利用以减少电子垃圾，但供应商控制的硬件安全机制在设备生命周期结束后是否仍有效尚不清楚，这方面在现有研究中被忽视了。

Method: 以开放硬件智能手机PinePhone为平台，模拟现实中的重利用场景，实验验证启动链完整性、TEE隔离性和硬件绑定密钥保护三个核心安全方面。

Result: 重建信任锚点非常复杂，供应商锁定机制阻碍了大多数废弃设备的安全再利用。

Conclusion: 需要制定安全重利用的要求，以解决供应商锁定问题，从而延长设备寿命、减少电子垃圾。

Abstract: An estimated 5.3 billion mobile phones became electronic waste in 2022. Many of these devices can be repurposed and used in different contexts to extend their lifetime and to reduce ecological impacts. An often overlooked aspect of smartphone reuse is cybersecurity: these devices embed hardware-backed security mechanisms that rely on vendor-controlled provisioning and are designed for a fixed device lifecycle. In this paper, we investigate whether security mechanisms and guarantees remain effective when devices are repurposed outside their original ecosystem. We explore security features in a PinePhone, an open-hardware smartphone, and focus on three core security aspects: boot chain integrity, isolation provided by the Trusted Execution Environment, and the protection of hardware-bound secrets. Our experiments simulate realistic repurposing scenarios and highlight the complexity of reconstructing trust anchors. We generalize our observations to infer requirements for secure repurposing and illustrate how vendor locked mechanisms hinder the repurposing of a majority of discarded devices.

</details>


### [136] [Steering LLM Viewpoints through Fabricated Evidence Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06244)
*Xi Yang,Chang Liu,Zhenglin Huang,Haoran Li,Weiming Zhang,Jian Weng,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Ghostwriter攻击框架，通过伪造可信证据诱导LLM采纳错误观点，实验表明商业模型高度脆弱，定制安全策略可达81%检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在决策中日益重要，但其对带有可信度标记的虚假证据存在认知脆弱性，可能产生误导性回复，对用户构成实质风险。

Method: Ghostwriter两阶段攻击框架：1) 将误导性陈述与捏造理由重新打包；2) 指令目标LLM在相关查询中采纳这些观点。在BBQ、ToxiGen及自建数据集上测试，并探索多种防御策略。

Result: 无外部安全分类器的商业LLM高度脆弱；前沿分类器防护模型（如GPT-5.4）可降低但无法消除攻击；定制安全策略使gpt-oss-safeguard达到81%检测率。

Conclusion: LLM对伪造可信证据存在系统性认知脆弱性，现有防御手段不足，但针对性安全策略可有效缓解。

Abstract: As chatbots increasingly influence daily decision-making, their potential to produce misleading responses poses substantial risks to users. This paper investigates a critical cognitive vulnerability in LLMs: their tendency to uncritically trust external context when presented with fabricated evidence bearing markers of credibility. We introduce Ghostwriter, a two-phase attack framework that first repackages misleading statements with fabricated rationales, then instruct target LLMs to incorporate these viewpoints when responding to relevant queries. Experiments on BBQ, ToxiGen, and our specialized dataset reveal that commercial LLMs without external safety classifiers remain highly vulnerable, while even frontier classifier-guarded models (e.g., GPT-5.4) reduce but do not eliminate the attack. Building on this, we explore multiple defense strategies, among which a tailored safety policy enables gpt-oss-safeguard to achieve 81% detection rate.

</details>


### [137] [SecRL-Prune: Structured Reinforcement Learning-Based Pruning of CodeLLMs for Preserving Adversarial Code Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06254)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Pooria Madani,Khalil El-Khatib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecRL-Prune框架，通过强化学习对CodeLLM进行结构化剪枝（压缩MLP/FFN通道），实验表明压缩后的模型仍能保留代码变异能力，可生成躲避签名检测的恶意软件变种，揭示了压缩CodeLLM的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CodeLLM在模型压缩后是否仍具备功能保持的代码突变能力（即生成多样化恶意软件变种以逃避签名检测），从而评估压缩部署下的安全风险。

Method: 提出SecRL-Prune框架：基于预训练教师模型，利用强化学习逐层学习剪枝策略，以教师-学生KL散度为奖励；通过缓存教师模型的top-P预测结果，避免教师和学生同时驻留GPU内存，提高效率。

Result: 在HumanEval上对三个7B CodeLLM进行10-30%压缩，SecRL-Prune在pass@k和var@k指标上持续优于现有结构化剪枝基线；对真实恶意软件样本的案例研究表明，20%剪枝模型生成的语义保持变异显著降低了检测率。

Conclusion: 代码变异能力能够在显著结构化剪枝下存活，压缩后的CodeLLM具有实际安全威胁，需引起重视。

Abstract: Large code language models (CodeLLMs) can generate and rewrite programs, enabling functionality-preserving code mutation that may be used to create diverse malware variants and evade signature-based detection. A key security question is whether this mutation capability survives model compression, which would make deployment feasible under limited hardware budgets. We propose SecRL-Prune, a structured pruning framework for CodeLLMs that operates on feed-forward (MLP/FFN) channels. Starting from a pretrained teacher, it learns a layer-wise pruning policy with reinforcement learning using a teacher-student KL-divergence reward. To improve efficiency, we cache the teacher's top-P predictions once and compare the pruned student against this compact target, avoiding simultaneous teacher-student residency in GPU memory. We evaluate SecRL-Prune on HumanEval using pass@k for execution correctness and var@k for code diversity across three 7B CodeLLMs at 10-30% compression. SecRL-Prune consistently preserves higher pass@k and var@k than recent structured pruning baselines under aggressive pruning. In a case study on real malware samples, semantics-preserving mutations from 20%-pruned models substantially reduced detections. These results show that code mutation capability can survive significant structured pruning, highlighting the security relevance of compressed CodeLLMs.

</details>


### [138] [Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06265)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Zahra Hashemi,Pooria Madani,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RESSAP框架，通过将单一分类器转化为选择性强化和增强的鲁棒集成分类器，结合特征选择（基于弹性度量）、随机子集推理和噪声数据增强，有效提升ML模型对对抗性逃逸攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据上的高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御机制在对抗逃逸攻击方面泛化能力有限，无法系统性地提升模型在多样化攻击场景下的鲁棒性。

Method: RESSAP框架：1) 将单一分类器转化为集成分类器；2) 每个分类器基于弹性度量（综合特征重要性和鲁棒性）选择特征子集进行训练；3) 推理时随机选择子集分类器进行预测；4) 训练中引入噪声数据增强强化决策边界。

Result: RESSAP显著提升了对抗逃逸攻击的鲁棒性，同时在干净数据上保持高准确率。

Conclusion: 该模型无关框架提供了一种可扩展、灵活的防御策略，无需对现有架构进行重大修改即可增强ML系统的安全性。

Abstract: Evasion attacks present a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning (ML)-based classifiers, particularly in critical applications such as fraud detection and cybersecurity. Although existing defense mechanisms are effective in some settings, they often suffer from limited generalizability and do not systematically improve model robustness across diverse attack scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors (RESSAP), a novel framework that transforms a single classifier into an ensemble of robust classifiers. Each classifier in the ensemble is trained on a carefully selected subset of features, where feature selection is guided by a resilience metric that accounts for both feature importance and robustness. During inference, a random subset of these classifiers is used to make predictions, increasing unpredictability and improving resistance to adversarial manipulation. In addition, noise-based data augmentation is applied during training to strengthen decision boundaries and improve generalization. Our experimental results demonstrate that RESSAP significantly improves robustness against adversarial evasion attacks while maintaining strong accuracy on clean data. Overall, this model-agnostic framework provides a scalable and flexible defense strategy for enhancing the security of machine learning systems without requiring major changes to existing architectures.

</details>


### [139] [Credential Disclosure in (EU) Digital Identity Wallets: Privacy Risks and Practical Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06354)
*Sheila Zingg,Daniele Lain,Yoshimichi Nakatsuka,Kari Kostiainen,Stefan Bechtold,Srdjan Čapkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对欧盟即将推出的EUDI数字钱包，研究了用户在凭证披露中的隐私保护问题。通过大规模用户与专家调查发现，用户普遍存在过度披露行为（如约20%用户向新闻网站披露官方ID）。作者提出的凭证助手（Credential Assistant）能将用户的错误披露率从约15%降至约7%，但仍无法完全消除风险，尤其对敏感属性需要更强干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟EUDI Wallet将于2026年底推出，允许用户在设备上持有数字凭证并向网站披露身份属性。尽管该系统带来诸多好处，但不良的凭证披露决策会引入隐私泄露、身份盗窃等风险。作者旨在研究用户凭证披露行为的问题并提出缓解方案。

Method: 作者开展了大规模用户与专家的凭证披露调查，并评估了他们提出的凭证助手（Credential Assistant）在展示专家建议和用户意见方面的有效性与可行性。

Result: 约20%的用户向新闻网站披露了官方ID，表明用户普遍难以保护隐私。凭证助手能将用户的错误披露率从约15%降低至约7%。然而，该工具未能完全消除不良的凭证披露决策，尤其是对敏感属性仍存在风险。

Conclusion: 用户的隐私保护能力不足将影响EUDI钱包的可用性并导致隐私侵犯和身份盗窃。凭证助手虽能显著减少错误，但仍需更强干预措施来彻底解决敏感属性的过度披露问题。

Abstract: The European Union will introduce the EUDI Wallet by late 2026, which allows users to hold digital credentials (i.e., representations of physical official identity documents) on their devices. This will allow users to securely and privately disclose identity attributes to websites. Although such a system has many benefits, it also introduces risks caused by poor credential disclosure decisions. In this paper, we (i) conduct a large-scale survey on credential disclosure with users and experts and (ii) evaluate the effectiveness and feasibility of our Credential Assistant that displays expert recommendations and user opinions. Our results show that users are likely to overshare (e.g., ~20% of users disclosed their official ID to news websites). This indicates that users struggle to protect their privacy, which will impact the usability of the EUDI Wallet and lead to privacy violations, identity theft, and other abuses of leaked credentials. Finally, we show that our Credential Assistant significantly reduces users' credential disclosure mistakes from ~15% to ~7%. However, it does not fully eliminate poor credential disclosure decisions, indicating that stronger interventions may be necessary, especially for sensitive attributes.

</details>


### [140] [WebMCP Tool Surface Poisoning: Runtime Manipulation Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06387)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Chia-Mu Yu,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现WebMCP协议中存在一种名为"会话中工具注入(MSTI)"的新型安全威胁，攻击者可利用第三方脚本在活跃会话中注入恶意工具，具体分为工具劫持与工具框架两种攻击方式，并提出了相应的缓解措施与安全设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebMCP协议允许网站直接向AI代理暴露工具，绕过了传统用户界面，引入了新的安全风险。特别是第三方脚本参与时，动态暴露的代理可访问工具会扩大Web会话的攻击面，亟需对这些新型威胁进行系统研究。

Method: 首先识别并定义了MSTI威胁，基于操作阶段和目标将其分类为工具劫持(Tool Hijacking)和工具框架(Tool Framing)。工具劫持通过AbortSignal API或工具注册时的竞态条件修改代理可见的工具集；工具框架则通过工具名称、描述、readOnlyHint和inputSchema等元数据字段影响代理对工具角色的感知。通过原型实现验证了两种攻击的有效性。

Result: 实验证明工具劫持和工具框架均能成功破坏WebMCP的预期功能。工具劫持利用竞态条件或AbortSignal API在工具注册阶段注入/移除工具；工具框架通过篡改元数据字段误导代理对工具功能的理解。

Conclusion: MSTI源于WebMCP独特的工具生命周期和结构化元数据设计，工具表面本身已成为新兴的安全关注点。提出了四条安全设计建议：将工具身份绑定到其来源、确保生命周期一致性、强制执行第三方工具的数据边界、维护工具注册和调用的可追溯日志。

Abstract: WebMCP is a newly emerging protocol that enables websites to expose tools directly to AI agents, bypassing traditional user interfaces and introducing new security risks. The dynamic exposure of agent-accessible tools in WebMCP expands the attack surface of web sessions, especially when third-party scripts are involved. In this study, we identify a new potential threat, termed Mid-Session Tool Injection (MSTI), in which attackers leverage third-party scripts to inject malicious tools during an active session. To better characterize this threat, we classify MSTI based on the stage and target of manipulation, distinguishing between Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing. Tool Hijacking modifies the set of tools visible to the agent through mechanisms such as the AbortSignal API or race conditions during tool registration. In contrast, Tool Framing influences the agent's perception of tool roles through metadata fields such as tool name, description, readOnlyHint, and inputSchema. Our implementation demonstrates that both Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing can successfully disrupt the intended functionality of WebMCP. Based on these results, we outline potential mitigation directions and provide security design recommendations for WebMCP, including binding tool identity to its origin, ensuring lifecycle consistency, enforcing data boundaries for third-party tools, and maintaining traceable logs of tool registration and invocation. These findings indicate that MSTI arises from WebMCP's unique tool lifecycle and structured metadata, making the tool surface itself an emerging security concern.

</details>


### [141] [Will the Agent Recuse Itself? Measuring LLM-Agent Compliance with In-Band Access-Deny Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06460)
*Thamilvendhan Munirathinam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了"Recuse Signal"——一种轻量级的、发布在协议已有信道上的合作性拒绝信号，类似于机器人排除协议（robots.txt）但用于实时访问控制。实验表明该信号在SSH场景下能100%诱导LLM代理主动退出，但最强大的模型在收到明确操作者授权框架后会选择继续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理持有真实凭证且无人工监管时，操作员缺乏标准方式告知代理某资源不可访问；现有访问控制要么放行（代理有合法凭证），要么硬拒绝（与其他客户端无异），缺少一种合作性的治理控制手段。

Method: 定义开放微标准，实现两个零/低足迹适配器（SSH横幅/PAM钩子和PostgreSQL线协议代理），在生产环境主机部署，进行受控实验（SSH；OpenAI GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini和Claude Code），对比有/无信号场景下代理的退出行为。

Result: 有信号时100%退避率 vs 无信号时100%任务完成率；但明确的操作者授权框架能使最强大的模型选择继续执行，而其他代理仍服从主机策略。

Conclusion: Recuse Signal是一种有效的合作性治理控制信号，但不是绝对的安全边界；不同模型对其响应存在差异，最强大的模型在明确授权下会覆盖信号。该标准、适配器和实验框架已开源供复现。

Abstract: As autonomous LLM agents increasingly hold real credentials and operate infrastructure without a human in the loop, operators have no standard way to tell an agent that a resource is off-limits. Access controls either let the agent in (it has valid credentials) or hard-fail it (indistinguishable from any other client). We propose a third mode: a lightweight, published in-band deny signal -- the Recuse Signal -- that a server emits over a protocol's existing channels (an SSH banner, a PostgreSQL NOTICE) asking a connecting automated agent to voluntarily withdraw. This is a cooperative governance control, the robots.txt analogue for live access; it is explicitly not a security boundary. Its value is entirely empirical and, to our knowledge, unmeasured: do compliant LLM agents actually honor such a signal? We define the signal as an open mini-standard, implement two zero- or low-footprint adapters (an SSH banner/PAM hook and a PostgreSQL wire-protocol proxy), deploy them on a live production host, and run a controlled experiment in which fresh agents are given a benign operations task and observed for recusal. In a pilot (SSH; OpenAI GPT-4o and GPT-4o-mini; and Claude Code as a deployed agent), the signal cleanly induces recusal -- 100% recusal when present versus 100% task completion in a no-signal control -- and, revealingly, behaves as a cooperative rather than absolute signal: an explicit operator-authorization framing flips the most capable model to proceed, while other agents continue to defer to the on-host policy. We release the standard, adapters, and experiment harness for reproduction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit r/ChangeMyView中一场被叫停的未公开AI生成账户现场辩论实验的数据集，发现AI代理在超三分之二的评论中使用了身份定位策略，几乎所有评论都包含结盟动作和权威声明，且大量触发认知偏差。与人类论证相比，AI代理在每个维度上都呈现相反分布：更密集的权威使用、更具对抗性的结盟、更依赖外部引用而非经验基础。研究表明在身份丰富的审议论坛中，真实与合成认知立场之间的界限日益模糊，仅靠信息披露无法解决这一不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析大型语言模型在未披露身份情况下如何在身份丰富的审议论坛中运作，探讨AI生成内容对人类认知和公共讨论的潜在影响，以及信息披露机制在应对这一问题上的局限性。

Method: 对Reddit r/ChangeMyView中AI生成评论语料库进行结构化内容分析，评估身份表现、权威信号、结盟策略和认知启发式激活模式，并与人类撰写的反驳论证进行对比分析。

Result: 身份定位出现在超三分之二评论中，结盟动作和权威声明几乎出现在所有评论中，认知偏差触发（尤其是确认偏差、代表性启发式和可得性启发式）占绝大多数。AI代理在权威使用密度、对抗性结盟强度和外部引用依赖度上均显著高于人类论证。

Conclusion: 在AI参与的环境中，真实与合成认知立场之间的区别日益模糊，信息披露本身无法解决这一不对称性。需要建立能够评估AI系统如何构建可信度（而不仅仅是检测其存在）的审计框架。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [143] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT（协议化动作-状态通信与传输）方法，将多智能体系统中的自由文本通信转换为紧凑的动作-状态记录，在不同拓扑结构下一致地改善了性能-成本权衡，在多个编程基准上实现了以更少token达到相同或更强的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，智能体间采用非约束自然语言进行通信会导致token消耗快速增长、共享上下文窗口被过度占用，进而影响系统性能和推理成本。现有研究中缺乏对通信策略的系统性分析以及针对性的优化方案。

Method: 首先分析五种常见智能体间通信策略在两种MAS拓扑结构下的表现，发现没有固定策略是普遍最优的，有效消息应保留下游智能体所需的动作中心信息。基于此提出PACT方法，将智能体间通信视为公开状态更新问题，在每个原始智能体输出进入共享历史之前，将其投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录。

Result: 在不同MAS拓扑结构下，PACT一致地改善了性能-成本权衡，以显著更少的token达到可比或更强的任务性能。在OpenHands上，PACT以-10% tokens-per-resolved提升了解决率；在SWE-agent上，PACT在保持解决率不变的同时将输入token减半。

Conclusion: 将自由格式的智能体间通信协议化为结构化的动作-状态记录，是提升多智能体系统效率和性能的有效途径，尤其在token预算受限的生产环境中具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [144] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架，通过检索开放网络证据生成背景知识，提升多模态模因的理解与检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略或依赖预训练模型的固定参数知识，这些知识对新兴模因可能不完整、过时或不可用。

Method: Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架：识别缺失知识→检索开放网络证据→综合证据生成背景知识，用于模因理解与检测。

Result: 在三个模因理解数据集和五个模因检测任务上，该方法在知识恢复、模因理解和下游检测方面均优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 通过动态检索外部知识弥补预训练模型的知识缺口，能有效提升对新兴模因的理解与检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [145] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对基于分块的时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）存在的上下文中毒问题（结构异常分块过度吸引注意力并降低零样本预测质量），本文提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，通过门控、路由和批评三个轻量级组件在推理时选择性抑制有害分块，无需更新模型参数。在53个GIFT-Eval数据集上对TimesFM 2.5进行评估，平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改进上限的89.9%。同时引入上下文敏感度曲线作为TSFMs的新可表征属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分块的时间序列基础模型存在上下文中毒问题：结构异常的分块会捕获不成比例的注意力，在零样本预测中悄无声息地降低预测质量。现有方法多聚焦于修改模型权重，而本文提出在推理时优化输入上下文来提升模型精度。

Method: 提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，包含三个轻量级组件：门控（Gate）、路由（Router）和批评（Critic），在推理时选择性识别并抑制有害分块，无需任何参数更新。

Result: 在53个GIFT-Eval数据集上对TimesFM 2.5进行K折交叉验证，GITCO平均降低MASE 1.95%，达到改进上限的89.9%。

Conclusion: 推理时上下文干预能有效提升TSFMs的零样本预测质量，并引入上下文敏感度曲线作为TSFMs的新可表征属性，该属性描述时间序列元特征到推理时上下文干预下期望精度提升的映射，由模型架构和数据统计结构共同塑造。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [146] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对循环工厂中返回产品的异质退化状态与剩余能力评估问题，提出了一种结合不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估的实例特定可靠性工作流。该框架以角磨机为案例，通过卷积编码器提取负载模式、LSTM预测功能变量（高斯均值与方差），并结合有限元应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估和Paris定律裂纹扩展分析进行疲劳评估。流式回放算法整合功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。测试显示平均2%容差准确率为0.9652，驱动电机电流和负载转速是最具挑战的动态输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PHM方法通常针对固定运行条件或孤立组件基准，材料疲劳评估很少与系统级功能预后相关联，导致循环工厂中返回产品的复用决策无法仅凭当前检测确定。

Method: 提出框架结合当前工具状态与近期力-扭矩使用窗口，使用卷积编码器提取主轴力和轴扭矩的负载模式，LSTM骨干网络预测九个功能变量的高斯均值和方差估计。同时，将负载历史通过有限元应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（含Haibach扩展）和Paris定律裂纹生长分析转化为输出轴疲劳信息。流式重放算法整合两个分支。

Result: 测试显示九个输出平均2%容差准确率为0.9652，热变量预测接近完美，驱动电机电流（R²=0.9750）和负载转速（R²=0.9924）是最具挑战的动态输出。扭矩历史对这些变量尤其重要，短历史场景下传统LSTM优于GRU和xLSTM。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效将组件级疲劳评估与系统级功能预后相结合，为循环工厂中异质退化产品的复用决策提供了可靠性校准依据，其中驱动电机电流的可靠性校准信息量最大。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [147] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SentinelBench，一个用于评估AI代理在长时间监控任务中表现的开源基准测试，包含100个跨10个合成网络环境的任务，衡量任务完成度、响应时间和资源使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理默认采用连续行动模式（持续调用工具、刷新页面等），这在需要长时间等待外部事件的监控类任务中效率低下。需要一种更好的策略——持续注意力（sustained attention），即代理监控环境，在外部事件使进展成为可能时及时响应，而不在等待时浪费资源。

Method: 构建SentinelBench基准测试，包含100个任务、10个合成网络环境（邮件、日历、金融、职业社交、娱乐等）。每个环境提供实时网络界面并回放脚本化事件序列，要求代理在状态动态变化的网页上导航和推理。测量任务完成度、响应时间和资源使用三个指标。

Result: 在三种模型和两种浏览器代理框架上进行了实验，建立了性能基线。结果表明代理设计选择会显著影响关键指标，SentinelBench能够区分代理行为中有意义的差异。

Conclusion: 长时间运行任务更适合采用持续注意力策略而非连续行动策略；SentinelBench可作为评估代理在时间演化监控任务中表现的标准化基准。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [148] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习（用于MRI预测MOAKS评分）与可解释统计建模（LCMM）的AI框架，通过不确定性量化过滤高置信度预测，将样本量扩展至2175个膝关节，发现骨髓病变、软骨丢失和半月板挤压是膝关节骨关节炎疼痛快速进展的重要风险因素（OR分别为1.62、1.83、2.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MOAKS评分依赖放射科医生手动标注，成本高、可扩展性差，且难以大规模研究膝关节结构异常与疼痛之间的复杂关系。现有深度学习方法缺乏预测不确定性估计，导致预测可靠性不足。

Method: 1）开发深度学习模型从MRI直接预测MOAKS特征；2）引入共形预测（Conformal Prediction）对预测结果进行不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测；3）使用纵向潜类别混合模型（LCMM）分析三种结构异常（BML、CART、ME）与四种互补性膝痛测量的关联，识别不同疼痛轨迹。

Result: 不确定性感知策略显著提升了三种结构异常的预测性能（BML的MCC从0.69提升至0.91，CART从0.45提升至0.80，ME从0.59提升至0.89）。LCMM分析识别出快速进展与稳定进展两种疼痛轨迹，快速进展组中BML的OR=1.62，CART的OR=1.83，ME的OR=2.50。

Conclusion: 骨髓病变、软骨丢失和半月板挤出是膝关节骨关节炎疼痛与功能进展的重要风险因素，所提出的可解释且可信的AI框架可在大规模研究中有效揭示结构与症状之间的关联。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>
